#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}

+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),

+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }

+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    double maxAbsolute = 0.0, maxRelative = 0.0;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + threadIdx.y + 16 * r;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + threadIdx.x + 16 * c;
+            if (row < n && col < n && row >= col) {
+                const double value = sums[r][c];
+                if constexpr (Operation == Product::Update) {
+                    output[row * n + col] -= value;
+                } else if constexpr (Operation == Product::Generate) {
+                    const double entry = value + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[row * n + col] = entry;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = entry;
+                } else {
+                    const double reference = original[row * n + col];
+                    double error = fabs(value - reference);
+                    if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;

+                                       error / (fabs(reference) + 1e-10));
+                    if (row != col) {
+                        const double upper = original[col * n + row];
+                        error = fabs(value - upper);
+                        if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                        maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                        maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                           error / (fabs(upper) + 1e-10));
+                    }

             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+
+    if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+        __shared__ double absolute[Threads], relative[Threads];
+        absolute[tid] = maxAbsolute;
+        relative[tid] = maxRelative;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int step = Threads / 2; step > 0; step /= 2) {
+            if (tid < step) {
+                absolute[tid] = fmax(absolute[tid], absolute[tid + step]);
+                relative[tid] = fmax(relative[tid], relative[tid + step]);
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }

+            errors[errorIndex] = absolute[0];
+            errors[errorIndex + 1] = relative[0];

-bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {

+// Solve each independent 64-row panel against the factored diagonal tile.
+__global__ void panelKernel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t base,
+                            int width, const int* failure) {
+    if (*failure >= 0) return;
+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double panel[Tile][Panel + 1];
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t firstRow = base + width + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    for (int item = tid; item < Panel * Panel; item += blockDim.x) {
+        const int row = item / Panel, col = item % Panel;
+        diagonal[row][col] = (row < width && col <= row)
+            ? matrix[(base + row) * n + base + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Panel; item += blockDim.x) {
+        const int row = item / Panel, col = item % Panel;

+            ? matrix[(firstRow + row) * n + base + col] : 0.0;

+__global__ void clearUpperKernel(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    if (blockIdx.x < blockIdx.y) return;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * 32 + threadIdx.x;
+    for (int r = threadIdx.y; r < 32; r += blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 32 + r;
+        if (col < n && row < n && col > row) matrix[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+    }
+}

+dim3 productGrid(size_t n) {
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    return dim3(tiles, tiles);

+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,

+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {

+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;

+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }

+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    double maxAbsolute = 0.0, maxRelative = 0.0;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + threadIdx.y + 16 * r;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + threadIdx.x + 16 * c;
+            if (row < n && col < n && row >= col) {
+                const double value = sums[r][c];
+                if constexpr (Operation == Product::Update) {
+                    output[row * n + col] -= value;
+                } else if constexpr (Operation == Product::Generate) {
+                    const double entry = value + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[row * n + col] = entry;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = entry;
+                } else {
+                    const double reference = original[row * n + col];
+                    double error = fabs(value - reference);
+                    if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                    maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                    maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                       error / (fabs(reference) + 1e-10));
+                    if (row != col) {
+                        const double upper = original[col * n + row];
+                        error = fabs(value - upper);
+                        if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                        maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                        maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                           error / (fabs(upper) + 1e-10));
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+        __shared__ double absolute[Threads], relative[Threads];
+        absolute[tid] = maxAbsolute;
+        relative[tid] = maxRelative;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int step = Threads / 2; step > 0; step /= 2) {
+            if (tid < step) {
+                absolute[tid] = fmax(absolute[tid], absolute[tid + step]);
+                relative[tid] = fmax(relative[tid], relative[tid + step]);
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            errors[errorIndex] = absolute[0];
+            errors[errorIndex + 1] = relative[0];
+        }
+    }
+}

+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                                   double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return;

+#if defined(__GLIBC__) && RAND_MAX == 2147483647

+        random.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n);

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:

+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}

+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);

+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;

+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    double maxAbsolute = 0.0, maxRelative = 0.0;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + threadIdx.y + 16 * r;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + threadIdx.x + 16 * c;
+            if (row < n && col < n && row >= col) {
+                const double value = sums[r][c];
+                if constexpr (Operation == Product::Update) {
+                    output[row * n + col] -= value;
+                } else if constexpr (Operation == Product::Generate) {
+                    const double entry = value + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[row * n + col] = entry;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = entry;
+                } else {
+                    const double reference = original[row * n + col];
+                    double error = fabs(value - reference);
+                    if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                    maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                    maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                       error / (fabs(reference) + 1e-10));
+                    if (row != col) {
+                        const double upper = original[col * n + row];
+                        error = fabs(value - upper);
+                        if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                        maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                        maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                           error / (fabs(upper) + 1e-10));
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+        __shared__ double absolute[Threads], relative[Threads];
+        absolute[tid] = maxAbsolute;
+        relative[tid] = maxRelative;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int step = Threads / 2; step > 0; step /= 2) {
+            if (tid < step) {
+                absolute[tid] = fmax(absolute[tid], absolute[tid + step]);
+                relative[tid] = fmax(relative[tid], relative[tid + step]);
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            errors[errorIndex] = absolute[0];
+            errors[errorIndex + 1] = relative[0];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Factor a small diagonal tile cooperatively. The enclosing stream orders
+// this dependency before the panel solve and the parallel trailing update.
+__global__ void diagonalKernel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t base,
+                               int width, int* failure) {

+        diagonal[row][col] = (row < width && col <= row)
+            ? matrix[(base + row) * n + base + col] : 0.0;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+

+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const double value = diagonal[k][k];
+            if (value <= 0.0) {
+                *failure = static_cast<int>(base + k);
+                failed = 1;

-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];

-

-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {

+        __syncthreads();

+            diagonal[tid][k] /= diagonal[k][k];
+        __syncthreads();
+

+                diagonal[row][col] = fma(-diagonal[row][k],

+__global__ void panelKernel(double* matrix, size_t n, size_t base,
+                            int width, const int* failure) {

+    __shared__ double diagonal[Panel][Panel + 1];
+    __shared__ double panel[Tile][Panel + 1];

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {

+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};

+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }
+}
+
+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    double maxAbsolute = 0.0, maxRelative = 0.0;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + threadIdx.y + 16 * r;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + threadIdx.x + 16 * c;
+            if (row < n && col < n && row >= col) {
+                const double value = sums[r][c];
+                if constexpr (Operation == Product::Update) {
+                    output[row * n + col] -= value;
+                } else if constexpr (Operation == Product::Generate) {
+                    const double entry = value + (row == col ? double(n) : 0.0);
+                    output[row * n + col] = entry;
+                    if (row != col) output[col * n + row] = entry;
+                } else {
+                    const double reference = original[row * n + col];
+                    double error = fabs(value - reference);
+                    if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                    maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                    maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                       error / (fabs(reference) + 1e-10));
+                    if (row != col) {
+                        const double upper = original[col * n + row];
+                        error = fabs(value - upper);
+                        if (!isfinite(error)) error = CUDART_INF;
+                        maxAbsolute = fmax(maxAbsolute, error);
+                        maxRelative = fmax(maxRelative,
+                                           error / (fabs(upper) + 1e-10));
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+        __shared__ double absolute[Threads], relative[Threads];
+        absolute[tid] = maxAbsolute;
+        relative[tid] = maxRelative;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int step = Threads / 2; step > 0; step /= 2) {
+            if (tid < step) {
+                absolute[tid] = fmax(absolute[tid], absolute[tid + step]);
+                relative[tid] = fmax(relative[tid], relative[tid + step]);
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            errors[errorIndex] = absolute[0];
+            errors[errorIndex + 1] = relative[0];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Factor a small diagonal tile cooperatively. The enclosing stream orders
+// this dependency before the panel solve and the parallel trailing update.

+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,

+    if (n == 0) return;

+

+        random.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n);

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Depth = 32;
+constexpr int Threads = 256;
+
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }
+}
+
+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }

+__global__ void clearUpperKernel(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    if (blockIdx.x < blockIdx.y) return;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * 32 + threadIdx.x;
+    for (int r = threadIdx.y; r < 32; r += blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 32 + r;
+        if (col < n && row < n && col > row) matrix[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+    }

+dim3 productGrid(size_t n) {
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    return dim3(tiles, tiles);
+}

+// A resident matrix lets the benchmark retain its generated input on the GPU.
+// Standalone calls still accept and return the original row-major host vector.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                           double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return true;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(matrix, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading Cholesky input");
+
+    DeviceBuffer<int> status(1);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemset(status.get(), 0xff, sizeof(int)), "Resetting pivot status");
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += Panel) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - base));
+        diagonalKernel<<<1, Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+        const size_t trailing = n - base - width;
+        if (trailing != 0) {
+            panelKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned int>((trailing + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+            productKernel<Product::Update><<<productGrid(trailing), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, base + width, base, width);
+        }
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching blocked Cholesky kernels");
+    int failure = -1;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(&failure, status.get(), sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "Reading Cholesky pivot status");
+    if (failure >= 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n",
+               failure);
+        return false;
+    }
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + 31) / 32);
+    clearUpperKernel<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(32, 8)>>>(matrix, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Clearing the upper triangle");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading Cholesky factor");
+    return true;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                                   double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    DeviceBuffer<double> random(n * n);
+
+#if defined(__GLIBC__) && RAND_MAX == 2147483647
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(size_t(256), (n * n + Threads - 1) / Threads));
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blocks) * Threads;
+    const uint32_t increment = advanceSeed(0u, 3 * stride);
+    const uint32_t multiplier = advanceSeed(1u, 3 * stride) - increment;
+    randomMatrixKernel<<<blocks, Threads>>>(random.get(), n * n,
+                                           multiplier, increment);
+#else
+    // Keep the platform's exact rand_r sequence on non-glibc systems.
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : B)
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(random.get(), B.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading random matrix");
+#endif
+
+    productKernel<Product::Generate><<<productGrid(n), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        random.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Generating positive definite matrix");
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading generated matrix");
+    // random's cudaFree waits for its outstanding uses before releasing it.
+}

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Depth = 32;
+constexpr int Threads = 256;
+
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }
+}
+
+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }

+__global__ void clearUpperKernel(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    if (blockIdx.x < blockIdx.y) return;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * 32 + threadIdx.x;
+    for (int r = threadIdx.y; r < 32; r += blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 32 + r;
+        if (col < n && row < n && col > row) matrix[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+dim3 productGrid(size_t n) {
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    return dim3(tiles, tiles);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// A resident matrix lets the benchmark retain its generated input on the GPU.
+// Standalone calls still accept and return the original row-major host vector.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                           double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return true;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(matrix, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading Cholesky input");
+
+    DeviceBuffer<int> status(1);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemset(status.get(), 0xff, sizeof(int)), "Resetting pivot status");
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += Panel) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - base));
+        diagonalKernel<<<1, Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+        const size_t trailing = n - base - width;
+        if (trailing != 0) {
+            panelKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned int>((trailing + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+            productKernel<Product::Update><<<productGrid(trailing), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, base + width, base, width);
+        }
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching blocked Cholesky kernels");
+    int failure = -1;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(&failure, status.get(), sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "Reading Cholesky pivot status");
+    if (failure >= 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n",
+               failure);
+        return false;
+    }
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + 31) / 32);
+    clearUpperKernel<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(32, 8)>>>(matrix, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Clearing the upper triangle");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading Cholesky factor");
+    return true;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                                   double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    DeviceBuffer<double> random(n * n);
+
+#if defined(__GLIBC__) && RAND_MAX == 2147483647
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(size_t(256), (n * n + Threads - 1) / Threads));
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blocks) * Threads;
+    const uint32_t increment = advanceSeed(0u, 3 * stride);
+    const uint32_t multiplier = advanceSeed(1u, 3 * stride) - increment;
+    randomMatrixKernel<<<blocks, Threads>>>(random.get(), n * n,
+                                           multiplier, increment);
+#else
+    // Keep the platform's exact rand_r sequence on non-glibc systems.
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : B)
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(random.get(), B.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading random matrix");
+#endif
+
+    productKernel<Product::Generate><<<productGrid(n), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        random.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Generating positive definite matrix");
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading generated matrix");
+    // random's cudaFree waits for its outstanding uses before releasing it.
+}
+

+            maxError = std::max(maxError, partial[i]);
+            relError = std::max(relError, partial[i + 1]);
+        }
+    }

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Depth = 32;
+constexpr int Threads = 256;
+
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }
+}
+
+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* __restrict__ input,
+                              double* __restrict__ output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }

+__global__ void clearUpperKernel(double* matrix, size_t n) {
+    if (blockIdx.x < blockIdx.y) return;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * 32 + threadIdx.x;
+    for (int r = threadIdx.y; r < 32; r += blockDim.y) {
+        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * 32 + r;
+        if (col < n && row < n && col > row) matrix[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+dim3 productGrid(size_t n) {
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+    return dim3(tiles, tiles);
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// A resident matrix lets the benchmark retain its generated input on the GPU.
+// Standalone calls still accept and return the original row-major host vector.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                           double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return true;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(matrix, A.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading Cholesky input");
+
+    DeviceBuffer<int> status(1);
+    checkCuda(cudaMemset(status.get(), 0xff, sizeof(int)), "Resetting pivot status");
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < n; base += Panel) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(size_t(Panel), n - base));
+        diagonalKernel<<<1, Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+        const size_t trailing = n - base - width;
+        if (trailing != 0) {
+            panelKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned int>((trailing + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          Threads>>>(matrix, n, base, width, status.get());
+            productKernel<Product::Update><<<productGrid(trailing), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+                matrix, matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, base + width, base, width);
+        }
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching blocked Cholesky kernels");
+    int failure = -1;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(&failure, status.get(), sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+              "Reading Cholesky pivot status");
+    if (failure >= 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n",
+               failure);
+        return false;
+    }
+    const unsigned int tiles = static_cast<unsigned int>((n + 31) / 32);
+    clearUpperKernel<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(32, 8)>>>(matrix, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Clearing the upper triangle");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading Cholesky factor");
+    return true;
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,
+                                   double* resident = nullptr) {
+    if (n == 0) return;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> storage(resident ? 0 : n * n);
+    double* matrix = resident ? resident : storage.get();
+    DeviceBuffer<double> random(n * n);
+
+#if defined(__GLIBC__) && RAND_MAX == 2147483647
+    const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+        std::min(size_t(256), (n * n + Threads - 1) / Threads));
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blocks) * Threads;
+    const uint32_t increment = advanceSeed(0u, 3 * stride);
+    const uint32_t multiplier = advanceSeed(1u, 3 * stride) - increment;
+    randomMatrixKernel<<<blocks, Threads>>>(random.get(), n * n,
+                                           multiplier, increment);
+#else
+    // Keep the platform's exact rand_r sequence on non-glibc systems.
+    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (double& value : B)
+        value = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(random.get(), B.data(), n * n * sizeof(double),
+                         cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "Uploading random matrix");
+#endif
+
+    productKernel<Product::Generate><<<productGrid(n), dim3(16, 16)>>>(
+        random.get(), matrix, nullptr, nullptr, n, 0, 0, n);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Generating positive definite matrix");
+    if (!resident)
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(A.data(), matrix, n * n * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "Downloading generated matrix");
+    // random's cudaFree waits for its outstanding uses before releasing it.
+}
+

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t result, const char* operation) {
+    if (result != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(result));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        if (count != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_),
+                                 count * sizeof(T)), "Allocating GPU memory");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return data_; }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+constexpr int Panel = 32;
+constexpr int Tile = 64;
+constexpr int Depth = 32;
+constexpr int Threads = 256;
+
+// glibc rand_r advances this 32-bit LCG three times per output. Skip ahead
+// with affine transformations so CUDA threads reproduce the serial seed stream.
+__host__ __device__ uint32_t advanceSeed(uint32_t seed, size_t steps) {
+    uint32_t multiplier = 1103515245u;
+    uint32_t increment = 12345u;
+    while (steps) {
+        if (steps & 1) seed = multiplier * seed + increment;
+        increment *= multiplier + 1u;
+        multiplier *= multiplier;
+        steps >>= 1;
+    }
+    return seed;
+}
+
+__global__ void randomMatrixKernel(double* matrix, size_t count,
+                                   uint32_t jumpMultiplier,
+                                   uint32_t jumpIncrement) {
+    const size_t id = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;
+    uint32_t seed = advanceSeed(42u, 3 * id);
+    for (size_t i = id; i < count; i += stride) {
+        uint32_t next = seed * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        uint32_t value = (next >> 16) & 2047u;
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        next = next * 1103515245u + 12345u;
+        value = (value << 10) ^ ((next >> 16) & 1023u);
+        matrix[i] = __dadd_rn(__ddiv_rn(static_cast<double>(value),
+                                       2147483647.0), -0.5);
+        seed = jumpMultiplier * seed + jumpIncrement;
+    }
+}
+
+enum class Product { Generate, Update, Validate };
+
+// Each 256-thread block computes a 64 x 64 tile, with a 4 x 4 register
+// accumulator per thread. Both operands are reused from shared memory.
+// Only lower tiles are evaluated; Gram products are symmetric.
+template <Product Operation>
+__global__ void productKernel(const double* input,
+                              double* output,
+                              const double* __restrict__ original,
+                              double* __restrict__ errors, size_t n,
+                              size_t offset, size_t begin, size_t length) {
+    const int tid = threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t errorIndex =
+        2 * (static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * gridDim.x + blockIdx.x);
+    if (blockIdx.y < blockIdx.x) {
+        if constexpr (Operation == Product::Validate) {
+            if (tid == 0) errors[errorIndex] = errors[errorIndex + 1] = 0.0;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    __shared__ double left[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[Depth][Tile + 1];
+    double sums[4][4] = {};
+    const size_t firstRow = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = offset + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < length; base += Depth) {
+        for (int item = tid; item < Tile * Depth; item += Threads) {
+            const int row = item / Depth;
+            const int k = item % Depth;
+            const size_t col = begin + base + k;
+            left[k][row] = (firstRow + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstRow + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+            right[k][row] = (firstCol + row < n && base + k < length)
+                ? input[(firstCol + row) * n + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll 4
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[4], b[4];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)
+                a[r] = left[k][threadIdx.y + 16 * r];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                b[c] = right[k][threadIdx.x + 16 * c];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c)
+                    sums[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], sums[r][c]);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
